# Commands · P · part 4

1,435 entries, pharn-plan to piv-cancel-loop. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pharn-plan**: Turn an Approved, unchanged SPEC.md into PLAN.md with its declared files and applied_lessons declaration (plus AC-TESTS.md for a templated SPEC). Run after the SPEC is approved, before /pharn-grill. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-plan.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-regress**: Check for regressions outside the feature: run the project's gates at the base commit and at HEAD and compare them (regression-report.json, REGRESSION.md). Run after /pharn-build. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-regress.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-review**: Review code with PHARN's review lenses run in parallel, then merge their findings into one findings.json and REVIEW.md. Standalone, not a pipeline stage. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-review.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-ship**: Run the whole pipeline for one feature when the user asks to ship it (spec, plan, grill, test, build, regress, verify), stopping at two human gates: SPEC approval and the merge/fix/abandon decision. `--quick` for a small change. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-ship.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-ship-close**: Part of /pharn-ship: the GATE-2 and STOP procedure it reads when a run ends. Not run on its own; use /pharn-ship. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-ship-close.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-ship-quick**: Part of /pharn-ship: the --quick deltas it reads at entry. Not run on its own; use /pharn-ship --quick. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-ship-quick.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-ship-stages**: Part of /pharn-ship: the stages it runs after the SPEC is approved, one section each. Not run on its own; use /pharn-ship. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-ship-stages.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-spec**: Turn a feature idea into SPEC.md: surface gaps, fill the SPEC template, stop for human approval (or, with --model-approve for /pharn-loop, the model approves), then pin it. The first pipeline stage; `--quick` writes a 1–3 criterion mini-SPEC. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-spec.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-supervise**: Decide an escalated full /pharn-loop run: proceed-at-risk, stop or (once) amend-spec, one reason per escalated grill finding, in SUPERVISOR.json, checked by supervisor.mjs. A full /pharn-loop runs it when triage escalates. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-supervise.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-test**: Write each acceptance criterion's test before the build, run them, require each to fail, and pin the evidence in AC-TESTS.lock.json. Run after /pharn-grill, before /pharn-build. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-test.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-triage**: Give every grill finding one disposition (proceed, replan or spec) in grill-triage.json and check it with grill-triage.mjs. Run after a full /pharn-grill; a full /pharn-loop runs it after its grill. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-triage.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-verify**: Verify a built feature: run the project's gates once at HEAD plus the acceptance-criteria gate (verify-report.json, VERIFY.md). Run after /pharn-build and /pharn-regress. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-verify.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **phase**: You are a phase execution enforcer. Run a solo plan-implement-review-closeout loop without team overhead. ([salexandr0s/ai-config](https://github.com/salexandr0s/ai-config/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/phase.md) · ★14)
- **phase**: Jump the idea-to-build session to a specific phase (1–6). Use sparingly — when the user types "/phase 4" (any phase 1-6) or explicitly asks to skip to a phase. ([winchxyz/idea-to-build](https://github.com/winchxyz/idea-to-build/blob/HEAD/distributions/claude-code-plugin/commands/phase.md) · ★12)

- **phase**: Audit pipeline: everything a phase has done to it — add one to a plan that already exists, run it end to end (every ready task, one at a time or as waves, then sign-off), pin which phase the pipeline reaches for first, or cancel one that will not be done. A bare `&lt;phaseId&gt;` runs it; --dry-run previews the run without mutating. ([AleksandarBisevac/claude-plugins](https://github.com/AleksandarBisevac/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/audit/commands/phase.md) · ★4)

- **phonology-examples-check**: `phonology-examples check` queues an advisory check of one saved Example Set revision against the grammar in the project's current [Baseline](term:baseline). For each example it asks PanGloss to generate surface forms from the underlying form in the example's declared context and reports whether the expected form was observed. A check never changes the FieldWorks project, never changes Readiness, ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-check.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-list**: `phonology-examples list` reads the current revision of each saved phonology Example Set from the project's Motif store. Example Sets are advisory evidence and do not affect Readiness or Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-list.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-results**: `phonology-examples results` reads the stored result of one phonology check: a page of per-example summaries, or one complete example. A check is advisory evidence about a saved Example Set against a [Baseline](term:baseline); it never changes Readiness and Apply ignores it. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-results.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-save**: `phonology-examples save` records underlying forms, expected surface forms and the grammar context for each example as an immutable revision, with an optional citation on each. A saved set is advisory evidence: it never changes Readiness and Apply ignores it. Saving does not change the FieldWorks project. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-save.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-show**: `phonology-examples show` reads one exact saved revision and returns its examples in authored order, including source context and any citations. Example Sets are advisory evidence and do not affect Readiness or Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-show.md) · ★1)

- **pipeline-drive**: Level 5b EXECUTOR of the autonomous pipeline driver. Headless (`claude -p`) layer that executes the SAFE, no-merge tier of a pipeline tick plan — route-foundational, drain-answer, drain-parse-miss, drain-clarification, retro-judge, and kind:spike drives — by invoking the real pipeline commands. STRUCTURALLY cannot merge: it is handed only the pre-filtered safe actions and is forbidden to open PRs, merge, or drive a kind:code item. Spawned by pipeline-drive.sh; the merging tier waits for level 5c. ([Towheads/temperloop_legacy](https://github.com/Towheads/temperloop_legacy/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/pipeline-drive.md))
- **pipeline-drive-merge**: Level 5c EXECUTOR of the autonomous pipeline driver — the MERGING tier. Headless (`claude -p`) layer that auto-executes the kind:code drives 5b deliberately leaves for the operator, by driving each through `/build --unattended` and letting /build's OWN timed/modal merge gate decide the merge. Spawned by pipeline-drive.sh under a merge-allowing containment overlay, gated by PIPELINE_DRIVE_MERGE=1 and a per-tick cap. It merges ONLY via /build's gated path — never by hand. ([Towheads/temperloop_legacy](https://github.com/Towheads/temperloop_legacy/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/pipeline-drive-merge.md))
